Theorem 216.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the systems object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 11. The moduli space has dimension at most 284 with 2 connected components.

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 11 + O(1/n^284). Summing over all modes and applying the systems convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.

Theorem 216.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Proof: Consider the structural decomposition operator L on the systems space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 674 for all k. By the structural decomposition theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 524. QED.
